
# CLAUDE.md

This file provides guidance to Claude Code when working with code in this repository.

## Project Overview

E2E test suite for the **Therapios** platform using Playwright. Therapios is a German healthcare management platform for physiotherapy practices. Tests cover three user roles — Admin, Therapist, and SuperAdmin — against both Staging and Production environments.

**Platform language:** German (UI labels, field names, and status values are in German)

## Commands

There are no custom npm scripts — run Playwright directly:

```bash
# Run all tests for a specific role (Staging)
npx playwright test --project=AdminJhen          # Staging Admin
npx playwright test --project=SandraZeibig       # Staging Therapist
npx playwright test --project=SAJhen             # Staging SuperAdmin

# Run all tests for a specific role (Production)
npx playwright test --project=AdminJhen-Prod     # Production Admin
npx playwright test --project=JhenQA-Prod        # Production Therapist
npx playwright test --project=SAJhen-Prod        # Production SuperAdmin

# Run a single spec file
npx playwright test tests/Staging/Admin/admin_search.spec.ts --project=AdminJhen

# Run by tag (mix and match role + feature tags)
npx playwright test --project=AdminJhen --grep "@CRMActivities"
npx playwright test --project=SAJhen --grep "@Abrechnung"
npx playwright test --project=SandraZeibig --grep "@uploadvo"

# Run headed (visible browser)
npx playwright test --project=AdminJhen --headed

# Re-generate auth state (do this when sessions expire)
npx playwright test --project=setup             # Staging auth
npx playwright test --project=setup-prod        # Production auth

# View HTML report
npx playwright show-report
```

## Playwright Projects & Auth

| Project | Role | Environment | Auth file |
|---|---|---|---|
| `setup` | — | Staging | generates all Staging `.auth` files |
| `AdminJhen` | Admin | Staging | `.auth/AdminJhen.json` |
| `SandraZeibig` | Therapist | Staging | `.auth/SandraZeibig.json` |
| `SAJhen` | SuperAdmin | Staging | `.auth/SuperAdmin.json` |
| `setup-prod` | — | Production | generates all Production `.auth` files |
| `AdminJhen-Prod` | Admin | Production | `.auth/AdminJhen-Prod.json` |
| `JhenQA-Prod` | Therapist | Production | `.auth/JhenQA-Prod.json` |
| `SAJhen-Prod` | SuperAdmin | Production | `.auth/SuperAdmin-Prod.json` |

- **Staging base URL**: `https://staging.therapios.de/`
- **Production base URL**: `https://app.therapios.de/`

| `admin_vorabinfo_footer.spec.ts` | `@Admin @LetterFooter @ReadOnly @Mutating` | RC 3.11.3 (#3525) the Vorabinformation hides the bank-details column (#3227) and the remaining four must fill the width evenly. **Deployed; AC1/AC3 pass, AC2 is `fixme`'d on a regression the fix introduced.** All geometry is read on a *derived* scale: the invoice footer's five columns measure 0/90.6/172.7/246.3/370.9pt, which over **453pt** is 0/20/38/54/82% — exactly the 20/18/16/28/18 the shared partial declares, so 453 is the measured content width. **The ticket's premise needs correcting:** it describes the bug as "4 columns totalling 72%, left-aligned", but the browser renormalised the declared 20/18/16/18 across the freed width (20/72=27.8%…), and every archived render through 2026-08-26 measures offsets **0/27.6/52.6/75%** — filling the width but UNEVENLY. Today: **0/25/50/75%**. A spec written to the "72%" wording would have asserted something that was never true. **Finding (AC2):** the even split NARROWED column 1 from 27.8% (126pt) to 25% (113pt), so "Curano Berlin-Brandenburg 2 GmbH" — one line on all 23 renders through 2026-08-26 — now breaks across two ("Curano Berlin-Brandenburg 2" / "GmbH") on all 3 renders from 2026-08-27. AC4 (Therapy Report) → `fixme`, no reachable PDF for this role. Uses `Pages/admin/admin.letter-layout.page.ts` |

| `admin_hardcoded_german_strings.spec.ts` | `@Admin @HardcodedGerman @ReadOnly` | RC 3.13 (#3611) the German interface still shows hardcoded English on several admin screens — labels written as plain strings in the component instead of going through `translate()`. **Partly fixed; the GKV table and all five named one-off strings are German, and the two biggest tables are not** → two `FINDING` tests. **A translation ticket has exactly one authoritative surface, the dictionary the deployed bundle ships** (#3337), but this ticket is the case where the dictionary is NOT enough: a hardcoded label is in NEITHER `de.json` nor `en.json`, so a flatten-and-diff is structurally blind to it — the only way to see one is to READ THE SCREEN and look for English. So the file does both: `literalCounts()` splits a bundle occurrence into "inside the two locale modules" vs "outside" (outside ⇒ some component hardcodes it), and `screenText()` reads the painted board. AC7 is clean on the distinctive phrases — `Created Date`, `Revenue (total)` and `My Notifications` each occur **1/1/0** (once, in the dictionary, nowhere else) — and **common words are useless for this**: `View` occurs 2896 times in the bundle, `Cancel` 382, `Actions` 168, so only a distinctive multi-word phrase can decide anything. Green: GKV-Abrechnung is fully German (`Erstellt am`, `Gesendet am`, `Aktionen`, `Umsatz (gesamt)`, `Stapelstatus`, `Ansehen`) with **no English survivor**, and collaterally `Batch-ID` / `IK Nummer` / `Bilder` went beyond the ticket's 9-item list; the upload dialog footer reads `Abbrechen` with no `Cancel`; and a **control test proves the key-labels are not rendering raw** — `columns.hono_doc` / `columns.hono_status` / the VA column resolve to `Hono Dok.` / `Hono Status` / `VA-Status`, which matters because **i18next returns a missing key verbatim**, so a plain-text label passed to `translate()` falls through unchanged and a "the label is German" assertion cannot distinguish the two. **Findings** → reported as green `FINDING` tests rather than `fixme`, because they are the ticket's own End Goal failing rather than an untestable AC: (a) **PKV-Abrechnung and Zuzahlungsverwaltung each still paint 20 English strings** (Invoice Nr, Invoice Date, Invoice Status, Invoice Amount, Therapist, Insurance Type, Overdue Date, Paid Date, Reminded Date, Sent to DC Date, To DC Date, Storno Nr, Storno Status, Storno DATEV Status, Billing Validation Status, VO Status, VO Nr, Revenue, Logs, Beh Status) — commit `55ad84cb1`'s own message admits it; (b) the **copayment tab's 10 invoice-status sub-tabs are English** (Not Sent, Sent, Overdue, Sent to Optica, Reminded, To Debt Collector, Sent to DC, Paid, Cancelled, On Hold) where the **PKV tab's 9 are German** — #2951 translated PKV only, so the same status vocabulary reads in two languages one tab apart. **Traps:** the bundle **escapes non-ASCII**, so a grep for `"Maßnahme hinzufügen..."` returns **0** and reads exactly like "never shipped" — `escapedCount()` converts to `Ma\xdfnahme hinzuf\xfcgen...` first, and every German assertion here goes through it; `openBillingTab()` matches the tab label by PREFIX because the labels carry live counts; and AC3's notification bell is an **icon-only header control with no testid and no accessible name** — a first attempt at locating it clicked nav links and navigated away, so AC3 and AC6 assert the dictionary + bundle references and *report* the on-screen state without asserting it (AC6's board painted `Maßnahme` inside 120 s on the passing run but not on an earlier probe even after 240 s). **AC6's strongest half is a negative:** the superseded wording `Handlungsaufforderung` has **0 occurrences in the whole bundle**, in either locale — the old column header is gone, not merely unused. Uses `Pages/admin/admin.hardcoded-german.page.ts` |
| `admin_vorabinfo_exemption_wording.spec.ts` | `@Admin @VorabinfoWording @ReadOnly @Mutating @Slow` | RC 3.14 (#3668, commit `9fd6fa547`) three corrected sentences in the Vorabinformation's copayment-exemption block — two reworded, one new — plus the same three in the Blanko variant (#3228's Ergotherapie and Physiotherapie letters). **Deployed; all six ACs verified on freshly generated letters. 7 passed, 0 `fixme`.** **A rendering ticket has ONE authoritative surface and it is not the archive:** a notice is archived at generation and **never re-rendered** (the ticket says so itself), so the archive can DATE the change but never confirm it — every AC assertion here runs on a letter the test generated. That also settles deployment, which `/status` cannot: these are Twig templates and it reports the release, not the commit (#3704). **THE TRAP THAT DELETES THE SENTENCE UNDER TEST:** `pdfText()` drops AC1's line entirely — it renders the callout as *“…die Rechnung erhalten Sie erst nach Sie haben eine Zuzahlungsbefreiung?”*, so a test written on it fails while the letter is correct. `pdfDocText()` (positioned runs, `pdf-layout.ts`) recovers it; the same extractor gap #3559's reminder letter hit, **pinned by its own test** so nobody re-diagnoses it as a rendering defect. **The AC's prose is not the shipped string, twice over:** AC1 quotes a comma where the template has an **EN DASH** (`&ndash;`, untouched by this ticket — only the ending phrase changed), so asserting the AC's literal sentence fails on a correct implementation (#3666's `÷`-vs-`/` again) — every sentence is matched on its PARTS, in order, via `containsInOrder()`; and **the email is entity-driven**, rendering `info@curano.de` for a Curano entity and `info@therapios.de` for a Therapios one (both occur across the three fixtures), so the address is matched as a pattern rather than the AC's literal. Verified: AC1/AC2/AC3 on the standard letter with **AC3's adjacency measured**, not just presence (the new sentence at offset 1147 against the instruction at 1074, at most one sentence end between them); AC4/AC5/AC6 on BOTH Blanko variants, with AC6 asserted as a four-part ORDER (`Dann zahlen Sie nichts.` → instruction → new sentence → `Eine Zuzahlungsbefreiung können Sie…`), since its position is the whole of that AC; and the superseded wording gone (`Senden Sie uns bitte eine Kopie an`, `Befreiungsausweis`, and `nach Behandlungsende` in the Blanko letters). **The #3522 interaction is the reason the fixtures are #3522's own:** the commit's note says the new sentence ran both the regular letter and the Ergotherapie Blanko to **two pages** until the paragraph gaps were tightened 5px → 3px, so the page count is asserted beside the wording — all three variants still fit **1 page** on patients 8474/8472/8468 (three prescribed treatments each). **The archive test is a CUTOVER scan**, and two data facts shape it: the oldest rows' S3 objects are **gone** (a 3-month-old signed file reads 0 bytes, the same 404 #3524 measured on Infoblätter) so the far end is unusable, and every run pushes its own three renders to the top so the recent window fills with post-fix letters — the scan walks back from the top, skips this run's ids and anything unreadable, and stops at the first old-wording letter. Measured: **notice 10235 (2026-09-14) is the last without the new sentence; 10236 (2026-09-19) the first with it.** **FINDING** → reported as a green test: **the standard letter now contradicts itself about the very phrase the ops team corrected.** AC1 updates the callout to “nach **Ende Ihrer Behandlung**” and AC4 updates the Blanko letter's final-invoice sentence to the same — but the standard letter carries **its own copy of AC4's sentence** ~500 characters below the callout (*“Sie erhalten die finale Rechnung nach **Behandlungsende**, berechnet werden dabei nur tatsächlich durchgeführte Behandlungen.”*) and it was not updated, so one letter says it both ways while the Blanko letter says it only the new way. Strictly out of scope per “does NOT change: any other paragraph of either letter” — a wording decision for the PM, not a defect. **Mutating**: each run generates three notices, archiving each patient's previous one — unavoidable, and the same write `admin_vorabinfo_one_page.spec.ts` makes. **Re-verified 2026-09-22 against the current build: 7 passed, 0 `fixme`** — three fresh letters (notices 10255/10256/10257), all three variants still 1 page, and the FINDING below unchanged (`Ende Ihrer Behandlung` at offset 1010, the stale `Behandlungsende` at 1539 in the same letter). `9fd6fa547` is still the newest commit touching the notice templates; the two later ones on that path are #3714's reminder branding and the password-reset work, neither of which goes near the exemption block. Uses `Pages/admin/admin.notice-wording.page.ts` |
| `admin_scan_selbstzahler_prefill.spec.ts` | `@Admin @ScanInsurancePrefill @ReadOnly @Slow` | RC 3.14 (#3710, commit `bbee3b6a5`) a "Selbstzahler" scan must stop pre-filling Privat Basis: the patient's own stored insurance type wins, the field is left empty with a hint when they have none, and a Blanko VO on a Privat Basis patient gets a second hint. **DEPLOYED 2026-09-17 — 7 passed, 2 `fixme`.** **This ticket is frontend-only, so `GET /status` cannot answer for it at all:** `bbee3b6a5` is an ancestor of `release/3.14.0`, the API reports `3.14.0`, and for a day both new i18n keys and both German strings occurred **0 times in the served bundle** while #3383's sibling key in the same dictionary section occurred **4** — the control that makes a zero mean something. The deployment test re-derives that verdict every run and the AC tests are gated on it, so the file flipped to asserting the fix with no edit when the bundle shipped (`entry-01bd9372…`, keys ×3, both German strings present). **Do NOT probe the bundle for `isSelfPayerScan`** — minification renames a function, so 0 proves nothing either way; keys and string literals survive. **The before/after is on record from one unchanged test:** image **10589** (a Selbstzahler scan for **Katharina Overath, stored PKV**) pre-filled **"Privat Basis"** on the pre-fix build, with the form flagging its own guess via #3383's badge *"Weicht von der Versicherung des Patienten ab"* — Flow telling the admin on the same screen that the value it chose disagreed with the patient's record — and pre-fills **"PKV"** with no badge on the deployed build. **The rule is a pure function, so both versions are ported and the painted value is compared against BOTH** (the #3704 dual-oracle technique), which is what lets each test assert the right thing on either build instead of needing a rewrite on deploy. **That also disqualifies the obvious fixture:** a Selbstzahler scan on a patient already stored `privat_basis` gives "Privat Basis" under either rule — 3 of the 9 candidates are like that, so a QA who picks one sees the correct value on a broken build; only a patient stored **PKV** separates them (6 images / 4 patients: 10310, 10589, 12132, 12133, 12134, 12650). **Fixture verdict over the whole 987-image pending-review pool:** AC1 **yes** (9 self-payer scans, 6 discriminating); **AC2 no** — all 9 match a patient who already has a stored type, so the empty-field case cannot be produced; AC3 yes but **not discriminating** (every named-insurer scan sits on a patient already stored PKV, so scan-wins and patient-wins agree); **AC4 yes and discriminating** — images **12342 / 12768** are BG scans on GKV patients, the one unaffected-path fixture that can actually fail if the change leaks past `privat_basis` (verified: the form paints BG, not GKV); **AC5 no** — all 8 Blanko scans are on GKV patients. **The implementation is wider than the Developer Reference predicted:** it names only `insurancePrefill.ts`, but `mapExtractionToFormValues.ts` needed the same guard — the mapper runs FIRST and writes `overrides.insuranceType` from the scan, so without it the resolver's new branch would be defeated by an already-filled field. **Traps:** `extractedData.insuranceType.value` is the **raw AI text** on older rows and the mapped enum on newer ones (`'Selbstzahler'` 7, `'Privat'` 2, `'GKV'` 330, `'public'` 117, `'PKV'` 11, `'private'` 6, `'BG'` 2, `'PKVH'` 1, none 511) — **`privat_basis` never appears in stored data**, so an API grep for it finds zero and reads as "no Selbstzahler scans exist" when there are nine; **`/patients?insuranceType=` is silently IGNORED** (every value returns all 8,384); the collection does **not** serialize `extractedData` and `groups[]` is ignored there, so the sweep costs one item read per image; and `getUsableScannedInsuranceType()` additionally gates on **confidence**, a third branch no AC mentions — an unusable confidence makes the scan contribute nothing, so neither the pre-fill nor the hint fires. **Two notes for the PM:** the ticket's Localization Reference records both German strings as "Pending", but the commit supplied them (`"Selbstzahler erkannt: bitte PKV oder Privat Basis wählen."` / `"Blanko-VOs gelten möglicherweise nicht für Privat Basis. Bitte den Heilmittel-Typ prüfen."`); and **AC5's shipped condition is a superset** — the hint keys on the Blanko switch's live value rather than on the scan having read a Blanko form, so manually toggling Blanko on a Privat Basis patient warns too, which is also the cheapest manual route to test it. AC2/AC5 → `fixme` on **two** independent walls each (not deployed, and no fixture), each recorded separately so the right one can be cleared first. **Read-only** — the VO form is opened and read, never saved. Uses `Pages/admin/admin.scan-insurance-prefill.page.ts` |
| `admin_letter_greeting_no_anrede.spec.ts` | `@Admin @LetterGreeting @ReadOnly @Mutating` | RC 3.15 (#3848, PR #3866 merged into `release/3.15.0` 2026-09-29) a patient whose **Anrede** is not Herr/Herrn/Frau must be greeted by **first AND last name** (“Sehr geehrte/r Bärbel Kramp,”) instead of by last name alone, and the Vorabinformation must name them the same way in its “Behandlung für …” line. **Deployed; 6 passed, 0 `fixme`.** About **390 of the 659** September Vorabinformationen went out with the last-name-only greeting. **A rendering ticket has ONE authoritative surface and it is not the archive** (#3668): a notice is archived at generation and never re-rendered, so the archive can DATE the change but never confirm it — every AC assertion runs on a letter this file GENERATED or on one rendered on demand, and `/status` cannot settle it either (release, not commit — #3704). **AC1 is driven as a CONTROLLED EXPERIMENT on ONE patient**, cycling QA patient **8474 Quincy TrugelloTest**'s Anrede through all four rows (`Frau` / `Herr` / none / the literal text `"null"`) and generating a fresh Vorabinformation each time — four patients with four different names would confound the rule with the names. `PATCH /patients/{id}` with `application/merge-patch+json` accepts all four values and the write is **self-restoring in a `finally`**; it is also the ticket's own QA step. Verified: `Sehr geehrte Frau TrugelloTest,` / `Sehr geehrter Herr TrugelloTest,` / `Sehr geehrte/r Quincy TrugelloTest,` for BOTH no-Anrede rows, with the “Behandlung für” line matching each. **The half that shows the CHANGE rather than a merely plausible letter:** on the two rows where the rule moved, the **pre-fix output is asserted ABSENT** (`Sehr geehrte/r TrugelloTest,` and `Behandlung für TrugelloTest`), with an anti-vacuity guard requiring at least two rows to separate the two rules — otherwise a table of four non-discriminating rows would pass against the old build. **AC2 is verified on three of the six letter types and the file says which:** the Vorabinformation and its **Blanko** variant (patient 8472, both lines, plus #3522's one-page rule re-checked since the longer greeting could have cost a page — it did not), and the **Zahlungserinnerung**, which is the cheap one because it is **rendered on demand and never stored** (#3559), so it needs no fixture preparation and **writes nothing**: invoice **R126-50** (patient 7708 Gisela Gerstenberg, no Anrede) reads `Sehr geehrte/r Gisela Gerstenberg,` against Herr/Frau controls. The remaining three (PKV, Zuzahlung, Storno) are **stored PDFs served as-is** (#3495) with **no preview route** (`/invoices/{id}/preview` 404s), so showing the new greeting on one means generating a new invoice for a no-Anrede patient — not done here; all six renderers read the same `DocumentRecipientResolver`, and the PR carries renderer tests for the copayment. **THE TRAP, and it manufactures a gender bug:** a letter whose patient has a **person-type billing address does not greet the patient at all** — it greets the guardian, which is AC3's path. The reminder for **Herr Karl Schäfer** legitimately reads **“Sehr geehrte Frau Schäfer,”** because his daughter **Susanne Schäfer** is the billing contact; read as a patient greeting that is a wrong-gender defect. Every fixture here is partitioned by `billingPerson()` FIRST, and the AC1 patient is asserted to have no person-type billing address before the experiment starts. **AC3** is verified on that letter (the guardian rule, not the patient rule, and the patient's own Anrede absent); its other half — a guardian **without** an Anrede keeping the full name — has **no fixture**, measured rather than assumed: **0 of 23** guardian contacts in the reminder population and **0 of 126** in a 1,800-patient sample carry no Anrede. **The Out of Scope is checked AND dates the deploy:** 8 archived notices belong to no-Anrede patients and **all 8 still carry the pre-fix greeting**, unrewritten — and the newest, **notice 10276 (2026-09-30 01:58)**, POST-DATES the PR merge (2026-09-29 23:45Z), so the cutover is the **deploy**, not the merge; partitioning on the commit date would misclassify it (#3512's lesson). **Population measured by pages spread across the id space**, because `/patients` embeds addresses and a 150-row page already costs ~25 s (#3373): of 1,800 sampled, **103 (5.7%) have no Anrede** → ~511 of 8,927 book-wide, against the ticket's 1,448 on production; **`salutation` is NOT a registered filter** (every value returns all 8,927), which is why the population needs a walk; and **no stored value outside Herr/Frau exists on staging**, so AC1's “any other text” row is reachable only by the write above — production has exactly one such patient. **Mutating**: six notices generated (each archives that patient's previous one, the same write `admin_vorabinfo_one_page.spec.ts` makes) plus the self-restoring Anrede round trips, all on QA `…Test` patients. Uses `Pages/admin/admin.letter-greeting.page.ts` (composing `admin.notice-wording.page.ts`) |
| `admin_cross_area_fee_validation.spec.ts` | `@Admin @CrossAreaValidation @ReadOnly` | RC 3.13 (#3576) VO creation/edit validation must flag a fee or Heilmittel whose treatment area differs from the VO's own Fachbereich. **Deployed and passing — validation id 54, `fee_area_matches_therapy_type`, severity `warning`, sort 42, category `remedy`, seeded by `Version20260901142128`; 13 tests, 1 `fixme`.** **Read-only, and the reason is the whole method:** `POST /prescriptions/preview-creation-validation` — the endpoint the CREATE form itself calls — hydrates a **transient** Prescription and, in its controller's own words, "does not read or write any PrescriptionValidation rows". So AC2's nine-row truth table is driven **exactly**, including combinations no VO on staging has, from a three-key payload (`therapyType`, `insuranceType`, `prescribedTreatments[].treatment` IRI). Omit `changedFields`: sending it (even `[]`) switches the backend to `evaluateAffected()` and re-runs nothing, which reads as "the check does not exist". Verified: AC2 **18 combinations, 0 mismatches** (the table driven once on a `treatment`-kind Heilmittel per area and once on a fee per area), each warning's German note compared against an independently rebuilt string so a check that flags the right rows but names the wrong ones still fails; AC1 (registered beside the other 20, the only new one, and one of exactly **two** warning-severity creation checks with `icd10_code_valid`); AC5; every mismatched row named and the correctly-aread one left out; `passiv` rows in scope **in both directions** (the implementation deliberately covers every `PrescribedTreatment`, not only `TreatmentKind::TREATMENT` like its sibling checks — AEB/KT-H-BV pin it); an **area-less catalogue row is not a mismatch** (ids 80 `<FEHLER>` and 103 `K` pass silently), nor is a VO with no Fachbereich or no prescribed rows. **The live population is surveyed, not sampled** — every catalogue row against both foreign therapy types, 266 `totalItems` probes: **29 VOs / 34 offending rows** spanning all three Fachbereiche and all four kinds (12 treatment / 11 one-time-fee / 8 per-treatment-fee / 3 passiv), 19 imported. Eight of them are re-evaluated through the form's own endpoint from each VO's OWN Fachbereich and prescribed rows, and all warn with every offending code named — VO **7209-9** (ergotherapy, carrying AB-P + KG + HBH-PT, all PT) is the three-mismatch specimen. **No VO carries a stored verdict for check 54** (7 of 10 sampled have verdicts at all, 0 for this check), because a verdict is only written when a VO is saved or re-checked — which is why AC1 has to be read off the endpoint rather than by hunting for existing amber rows. **THE TRAP, and it silently kills a third of any survey:** `TherapyType` is `physiotherapy` / **`ergotherapy`** / `speech_therapy` — NOT `occupational_therapy`, which `/prescriptions?therapyType=` accepts **silently** and answers `totalItems: 0` for, exactly like a nonsense value (`ergotherapy` answers 9,113). The first test proves the vocabulary partitions before any zero from this endpoint is believed. **Also note `treatment.area` has exactly three values** (PT 79 / ERGO 43 / SSSST 11 of 135, 2 area-less) — the Developer Reference's sub-area worry (`PT_ZAE`, `ET_NUT`, `PO`) is about `remedy_catalog.therapyArea`, a column the check does not read, so there is no roll-up decision to make; and **`SSSST` is five S** — `TreatmentAreaEnum` read `SSST` until this ticket needed it, because nothing had ever compared against the speech-therapy case, so anything that did ask silently answered "not speech therapy". Reading a VO's prescribed rows needs care too: `groups[]` **replaces** the default set (`overrideDefaultGroups: true`), so `groups[]=billing:read` returns `therapyType: null`, and an embedded `treatment` carries `@id` but **no bare `id`**. **Finding** → the check **does not apply to a Privat Basis VO**: #3608 (`Version20260903140041`) gave every PKV check the `privat_basis` insurance type "so the two types always evaluate the same set", and id 54 — seeded two days earlier — is the **only one of the 7 PKV creation checks that did not get it**. Verified behaviourally: the same shape that warns under public/private/accident is **not evaluated at all** under `privat_basis` (6 other checks still run, so it is not an empty set). **AC3/AC4 → `fixme`, a PM question rather than a defect:** severity is display-only. `summarizeStates()` requires `failed === 0` for `allPassed` and `VoForm.handleSave()` submits only when `allPassed`, while the panel merely splits warnings out of the count with the comment "Split out for display only … so saving behaves exactly as before" (#3342) — so a cross-area warning leaves "Speichern" inert exactly as an error does, against AC3's "not a blocking error — the admin can still save" and the migration's own "the check never blocks a save". AC4's acknowledge route (the panel's per-check "Bestanden" toggle, which flips the check and makes `allPassed` true) does work. Not asserted end-to-end because observing the save gate means driving Create-VO to completion on a real patient. Uses `Pages/admin/admin.cross-area-validation.page.ts` |

Also asserts no patient data in that context. `largest[]` arrives as `[Object]` and Sentry is currently rate-limiting **error** events org-wide → both `fixme`'d with evidence; W1 (offline mutations survive eviction) is `fixme`'d as write-heavy. | `therapist_untreated_days.spec.ts` | `@Therapist @TBoardV2 @UntreatedDays` | RC 3.11.1 (#3471) "Tage seit Beh." is a property of the PATIENT, not of a VO row. **Read-only** — it reads the board's own `GET /therapist-prescription-groups` response as the page loads it (never a hand-built query, so the rows asserted are exactly the rows painted) and drives filter/sort/hint controls only. Asserts the rollup over the whole caseload: every VO row of a patient carries one value (0 of 17 multi-VO patients disagree), that value is the patient's most recent treatment on a **non-terminal** VO recomputed independently from `lastTreatmentDate`, and a patient with no treatable treatment reports **no value at all** — the API OMITS the field (so it reads `undefined`, never `null` or `0`) and the column paints a dash "–" (51 of 79 rows on this board). The 14+ hint reads "6 Patienten seit 14+ Tagen nicht behandelt" against 7 qualifying rows — the dedupe visible in one number; the BEHANDLUNGSLÜCKE option "Seit 14+ Tagen unbehandelt" previews and paints exactly the 7 rows, all ≥14; sorting the column orders by the corrected value (a dash sorts as 0, `?? 0`) and never splits a patient's rows apart. **Traps:** the applied-filter chip bar grows its OWN "Alle löschen", which `openFilterPanel()` mistakes for an open panel — with `actionTimeout` at 0 the click that follows hangs the whole test, so `clearGapFilter()` opens the panel by hand; and a served value must be compared against ONE resolved "today" (`resolveToday()`), or two treatment dates a day apart both "agree". **Finding:** the fix excludes **Abgelaufen and Abgebrochen** on top of the ticket's three statuses — 6 of Sandra's 49 patients (e.g. 8259 Lisa MontanaTest, 947201-1 [Abgebrochen] 2026-07-20) read "–" where AC2 as written yields a real gap, so they can never reach the hint → `fixme`'d for the PM. Uses `Pages/therapist/therapist.untreated-days.page.ts` |
| `therapist_sync_retry_recovery.spec.ts` | `@Therapist @SyncQueue @ReadOnly` | RC 3.12 (#3594) a temporary server problem (a 409 collision with another save, or a 5xx outage) must be retried automatically instead of being filed under "Nicht gespeichert", and the startup recovery notice must count only entries still waiting to send. **Deployed; all seven ACs verified live.** **The ticket declares two of its own cases untestable and both are one-liners here:** AC1 is "hard to provoke by hand … there is no manual staging repro — do not attempt to force one" and AC2 wants an emulator with its DNS pointed at a dead address. But `packages/dataprovider` is shared with the WEB build — the queue persists to `localStorage` under **`dataprovider/queue/v1`** (`{version:1, entries:[{id,resource,type,payload,state,attempts,createdAt,updatedAt,lastError}]}`, states `pending|syncing|retrying|failed|done`) and the replay executor writes to **`POST /activities`** (not `/activities/bulk`), so `page.route()` returns the exact 409/503/201 each AC describes on demand. **Nothing reaches the server:** every write is answered by the test, so no Activity is created — and the queue is SEEDED with `addInitScript` rather than produced through the Doku modal, which makes AC6's "2 waiting and 1 filed" a literal fixture instead of the ticket's six-step offline recipe, and skips the suite's flakiest surface entirely. Verified: AC1/AC2 both trace `pending → retrying → pending` and **never** touch `failed`, with **10** retries the app made on its own and the entry left at `attempts: 0` (`markPending(resetAttempts)`); AC3's two entries reach `done` untouched and the bar goes to "alles gesendet"; AC4 3 pending → "(3)"; AC5 only-filed → **no notice at all**, panel intact with Grund / "Erneut versuchen" / "Verwerfen"; AC6 → **"(2)"**; AC7 422 → `failed` with `reasonKey: sync.failure.validation`. **The trap that decides whether this ticket reads as shipped:** the Developer Reference says "409 currently sits in the permanent set and needs to move to the transient/retry path" — **it never moved.** `PERMANENT_STATUSES` in the served bundle is still `new Set([400,401,403,404,409,422])`, and a probe written to that wording concludes the fix is missing. What shipped is an escape hatch inside `handleFailure` (`const isConflict = 409 === e.status; return 'permanent' !== cls || isConflict ? …retry… : markFailed(…)`), which deliberately leaves the classification alone so 409 still maps to `sync.failure.conflict` ("Termin bereits dokumentiert"). The persisted entry shows the divergence directly: `state: "pending"` with `lastError.class: "permanent"`. **Second thing to hold onto: the sync bar and the notice are SUPPOSED to disagree.** `counts()` gained `awaitingSend = pending+syncing+retrying` beside `unsynced = …+failed`; the bar reads `unsynced`, `useRecoveredWorkNotice` reads `awaitingSend` and fires only above zero. Seeded 2 pending + 1 filed, one screen reads "3 Änderungen nicht gesendet" AND "(2)" — a test expecting them to agree reports a defect that is not there. **Other traps:** `hydrate()` runs `purgeExpired()` (30 days by `createdAt`) and rewrites `syncing` → `pending`, so seeds must be stamped `Date.now()`; the notice is a **toast** fired once per engine mount behind a `useRef` latch, so it must be polled from the first paint; `innerText` splits the bar into "Stand von vor 0 Min" and "· N Änderungen nicht gesendet" as separate lines, which defeats `TherapistBoardV2Page.syncStatus()`'s single-line match; and AC3's "the bar started out showing work" is **not reliably samplable** — with the server answering the queue can drain inside one 250ms poll, so that precondition is taken from the notice count and the seed instead. **Run at `--workers=1`** — each test mints a session with `POST /auth`, which #3462 throttles at 5/min per username; in parallel every test fails at login for a reason unrelated to this ticket. **Observation for the PM** (copy predates #3594, so not an AC failure): during an outage the Sync-Status panel's only line is "Alle Änderungen synchronisiert" while the bar above says 2 unsent — rare before, because a stuck entry was filed quickly and the panel then had something to show, but now the normal outage state by design. Also "1 Änderungen nicht gesendet" gets the German plural wrong at N=1, and the ticket's own Localization Reference carries the same string. Uses `Pages/therapist/therapist.sync-queue.page.ts` |

| `sa_flow_boards_load_time.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @FlowBoards @BoardTiming @ReadOnly` | RC 3.12 (#3401) every Flow Board section must load within 10 s (design target 3 s) and never fail from slowness. **Deployed in `e87c91bad`; a clear improvement, but AC1/AC2 still fail on one section** — both `fixme`'d with the numbers, with a green evidence test that re-measures and prints the whole table each run. **Method is the point:** a repeat of the SAME window measures the ~5-minute server cache (cold 6.7 s → warm 0.45 s), so every sample uses a DISTINCT cold window, and medians are taken over three of them — because staging varies enormously: `/kpis/orga/risks` measured **21.0 s busy and 5.6 s quiet** 40 minutes apart. **What does NOT move with load is the Monat trend**: `/kpis/management/trend?level=monat` medians **26.9 s busy / 26.6 s quiet** (max 30.3 s) and `/kpis/management/orga-trend` at monat **28.8 s** — sitting on top of the two coincident 30 s ceilings, which is how AC2 breaks: that request returned **504** twice (ALB idle timeout) and in the browser was **aborted at 29,996 ms** — exactly `KPI_READ_TIMEOUT_MS`, which `BoardTimingPage.clientReadTimeoutMs()` reads out of the deployed bundle — with **"Daten konnten nicht geladen werden"** on screen. Timing the abort is what rules out a re-render cancelling its own request. A third full pass summarised as **35 combinations, 2 over the 10 s ceiling, 12 over the 3 s target** — the 2 being those same Monat trends. The ticket's own case improved: `/kpis/admin-performance` from a recorded 15–29 s to a 7.5–8.1 s median, the whole Admin-Performance board mounting for Monat July in ~12 s. Green: the regression guard over six section/granularity pairs with headroom, AC2's loading-state half, AC5 (a granularity click with 5 requests in flight acknowledged in 143 ms), AC3's one deliberately-moved figure (VO 29506 worklist revenue **2694.6**, pre-fix 2689.55 — the ORM-vs-SQL hydration difference the commit documents), and AC4 read-only via TTL (cold 6.7 s → warm 0.45 s → 5.2 s again after 6 min, so staleness is bounded well inside the 15-minute window; the response carries no cache headers, so timing is the only way to see it). AC3's pre/post comparison → `fixme` (no pre-fix baseline exists on staging any more). **Run at `--workers=1`** — parallel tests contend and inflate each other's timings. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.board-timing.page.ts` |
| `sa_flow_boards_date_basis.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @FlowBoards @DateBasisNote @ReadOnly` | RC 3.12 (#3400) both Management revenue charts must say they bucket by TREATMENT date. **Deployed; all 4 ACs pass.** It shipped inside commit `e87c91bad` as `ChartDateBasisNote`, **not** through its own PR #3455 (whose diff had collapsed to a lint fix by merge time) — looking for it under the PR finds nothing. Each chart header carries the subtitle **"nach Behandlungsdatum"** plus a click-to-open explanation, and the component is one of the few things on this board with real hooks: `[data-testid="date-basis-info"]` (a genuine `role="button"`, `tabindex="0"`, accessible name = the subtitle) and `[data-testid="date-basis-close"]`. Asserted against the failures the ACs forbid, not just presence: **hover does nothing** at 1920 and at 1024 (the hover-tooltip the ticket rules out), click/Enter/Space all open, and Escape / the close control / clicking away are each exercised from a fresh open. AC1's "directly under the title" is **measured** — title bottom → subtitle top is **2px** with left edges flush (x=57) on both charts. **Traps:** the popover is a react-native-paper `Menu` in the app-root portal, so it is NOT inside the chart card — locate it by text; its text also propagates up to the full-screen backdrop (0,0 → viewport) and down into 1px wrappers, so measure LEAF nodes and take the widest (264px); and Paper ANIMATES it in, so a box read the moment the text appears catches it mid-flight (`x=-98, w=0` at 500px, `x=57, w=312` a second later) — `dateBasisPopoverBox()` polls for two matching readings. Two clicks here are timing-sensitive and both are handled in the page object rather than by luck: the board repaints as its five aggregations land and swallows a click that arrives mid-repaint (`openDateBasis()` waits for the board and retries), and the close control rides in with the open animation, so a click dispatched while it is still moving misses it and the popover stays open — which reads as "the close control is broken", and only under load. Green at `--workers=2` with those waits. Also verified: the popover stays fully on screen at 500px (x=69→333), the regression the anchor-on-the-row fix addressed. **Finding** → `fixme`: the trigger sets `accessibilityState={{expanded}}` but **no `aria-expanded` reaches the DOM** — attributes are byte-identical open and closed, so a screen reader is not told the popover opened. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.flow-boards.page.ts` (`DATE_BASIS` section) |

| `sa_vo_validation_choice.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @ValidationChoice @CreationValidation` | RC 3.11 (#3340) an admin's explicit "Zur Korrektur speichern" / "Speichern" choice must not be silently overridden. Recompute is re-fired by flipping `actionRequired` — a re-check POST persists nothing when verdicts are unchanged. **Runs as Super Admin: Admin gets 403 PATCHing these VOs.** **Finding:** "Speichern" is inert (no dialog, no request) on a VO with failing checks → `fixme` |

The window 2026-06-30…08-18, which contains Wed 2026-08-12 (the day the PM records running `app:transition-to-order`), holds **no** move into Bestellen at all, so the PM's AC2 evidence (CRM 155→177) is not corroborated by the data. Not proof of a defect — the fix ships with unit tests over all 7 weekday scenarios — but #3299 is not re-verifiable here until the nightly job runs on a Wednesday against populated Individual lead times (all 1,459 practices are 21d/Standard, see `sa_order_flag_reset`). **Traps:** scope to the ship date or the 255 pre-fix Thu–Sun moves read as violations of a rule that did not exist; exclude `meta.type: manual` or ordinary Thursday admin work reads as failure; and the 851k-row log **must** carry its `type` filter — the unfiltered collection answers **504** from page 2. | `sa_datev_old_format_push.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @DatevOldFormat @ReadOnly` | RC 3.11.1 (#3440) PKV invoices whose number predates the `R` format must be delivered to DATEV **under their existing number**. **Read-only — every request a GET.** The delivery is a console command, `app:datev:push-legacy-pkv-invoices` (preview by default, `--force` to execute), run as a one-off Fargate task on `therapios-staging-console`. **Staging is preview-only by decision** — `DATEV_SYNC_ENABLED=false` makes `--force` abort at the guard, and that same SSM parameter also sets the State of the three DATEV EventBridge rules, so enabling it would start the nightly push/pull/debtor-creation against staging data on a Riecken gateway whose URL and credentials are **byte-identical to production** (only test Mandant 9999 separates the traffic). The real run happens on production. AC1 derives the population from the ticket's own rule and lands **set-identical to the command's 2026-08-24 preview**: **44 old-format PKV invoices, EUR 32,968.57, 0 skipped — 43 `overdue` ("Ausstehend") + 1 `to_send_to_dc` ("Inkasso", `126-4` on VO 91-2, €78)**, cross-checked on both count and total so a quietly widened rule breaks the sum. Two boundary cases the rule must exclude and does: **7 Storno documents (`S126-1`, `S426-2`, …) also fail "starts with R"** (the command excludes them explicitly — stornos have their own sync path) and **40 old-format GKV invoices** satisfy the number rule, so the insurance scope is load-bearing. AC2's pre-state is asserted from both surfaces: all 84 invoices in DATEV are current-format, each of the 44 has no `datevSyncStatus`/`datevSyncedAt`/`datev_push_success` log, and `datev_push_failed` is 0 — the exclusion is silent, nothing was attempted. AC5 is **structural, not observable**: `findPendingDatevSync()` (the nightly PUSH selection) is untouched and still `R`-only; only `findUnpaidForPaymentMatching()` widened, from `LIKE 'R%'` to `LIKE 'R%' OR datevSyncStatus = SYNCED` — so an old-format invoice becomes pollable *only* after the one-time push sets that status, and nothing is marked "already delivered". AC3 has **no fixture** — all 44 are unpaid. **Traps:** `datevSyncStatus`/`datevSyncedAt` are **omitted from the payload until an invoice is actually pushed**, so "not synced" reads `undefined` and `?datevSyncStatus=pending` answers **0**; `datevSyncAttempts` is a *failure* counter (0 on all 534) and cannot stand in for "was this pushed"; insurance type lives on the prescription, not the invoice; `batchId` is unique **per push, not per run**; and the 44 sit on archived VOs, so the UI needs `pkvBilling[treatmentStatus]=Archiviert` (#3277). **Finding (#2856, not #3440, but it blocks this ticket's QA route):** PKV-Abrechnung's `DATEV: Ausstehend` filter returns **0 rows in every combination** while the DATEV column renders "Ausstehend" for every unsynced row — a PM cannot list the affected invoices from the UI. **Runbook:** the nightly does not retry what the one-time command defers, so re-running the command is the only retry path — safe to repeat because its last selection clause is `datevSyncStatus IS NULL OR = FAILED`. The two post-run tests (AC2/AC6 and AC4) are data-gated and **run unchanged against production** after the real delivery. AC1/AC6 preview+CSV, AC3 and AC5 → `fixme` with the verified out-of-band evidence recorded inline. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.datev-old-format.page.ts` |

| `sa_pkv_invoice_fee_lines.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @PkvInvoiceFeeLines @ReadOnly @Mutating` | RC 3.12 (#3533) a PKV invoice's printed line-item table must sum to its own printed total, with every fee counted in the total shown as its own line. **The fix IS deployed on staging, and the before/after pair proves it:** two VOs of the same shape (`KG-H`+`HBH-PT`+ an **unmaterialised `AB-P`**) — **R426-36** (inv 296) rendered **13.07.2026** prints two lines and NO AB-P line, while **R426-93** (inv 629) rendered **28.08.2026** prints the same two plus `1 x Verwaltungsaufwand Arztbericht`. **The mechanism:** carrying a one-time fee is not what puts a VO at risk — the fee must also be **unmaterialised**, i.e. have no `ActivityTreatment` row. Matched pair, both carrying exactly `MLD45H`+`HBH-PT`+`AB-L`: presc **23602/R526-55** has rows `{MLD45H:6,HBH-PT:6,AB-L:1}` and prints `1 x AB-L 9,30`; presc **27764/126-5** has `{MLD45H:10,HBH-PT:10}` and **no AB-L row**, so the calculator still charges the 9,30 (a one-time fee is attributed to the VO's first-ever activity) while the pre-fix renderer, walking `activity->getActivityTreatments()`, could not print it → **953,10 against a printed 962,40**. Predicate `one_time_fee ∧ ¬materialised` is predictive: of **132** PKV invoices, 11 carry a one-time fee, **3** have it unmaterialised, and exactly the **2** with a non-zero tariff show the gap (126-5, 426-15) — the ticket's own count. **Only `AB-L` (9,30) and `L-ED` (150,00) carry a non-zero PRIVAT tariff**; the other seven one-time fees are 0,00, so they can vanish from a table without moving the total — which is why the deployment pair demonstrates the LINE APPEARING (AC1's literal wording) rather than a gap closing, and why L-ED is the 150,00 EUR ceiling on the worst silent gap. **Compare table sum against the PRINTED total, never `totalRevenue`**: `getInvoiceAmount()` prefers the issue-time snapshot (#3093), so R426-70/R526-59 are CONSISTENT documents 78,00 and 212,60 below their VO's live revenue and would be false positives. **Traps:** the invoice prints a fee by DESCRIPTION, never by code — matching on the code only works for `AB-L` (whose description IS "AB-L"), while `AB-P`/`AB-E` both print as "Verwaltungsaufwand Arztbericht"; `GET /activities?prescription.id=` is silently ignored (use `?prescription=`); regenerating an issued invoice answers **400 "A cancellation reason is required to regenerate an issued invoice."**; and `parseInvoice` retries once because a torn download parses to a null total, which `isInternallyConsistent` would otherwise report as a huge gap. **Non-discriminating fixtures pinned by a test:** R426-92 (VO 8091-2) carries no one-time fee, and R526-69 (VO 8922-1) has its AB-E materialised AND worth 0,00 — both sum correctly on either build, so neither is evidence. **Findings** → `fixme`: R426-36 keeps its pre-fix document (AC4) and its zero-tariff fee hides the real exposure (3 VOs structurally affected, not 2 — and #3378 could backdate a tariff onto it); R426-57/R526-18/126-12 print a table **LARGER** than their total (+41,80/+41,80/+24,00), the mirror image no AC describes; 226-2/326-43 are short by whole **treatment** lines (78,00 and 312,00, the two largest gaps) on VOs with no fees at all. Uses `Pages/superadmin/sa.pkv-invoice-lines.page.ts` |

The one client-reachable way is a NEWLY created invoice — `POST /prescriptions/{id}/generate-invoice` on a copayment-eligible VO with none writes the PDF from an async worker, so `pdf_path` is NULL for ~30 s. VO 8869-2 → invoice 630 (R726-24): **409 `"No stored PDF exists for this invoice."` at +2.6 s, stored at +28 s**; VO 3277-4 → 631 (R126-120), and a bulk of `[630 stored, 631 missing]` returned `Zuzahlung_Wieland_R726-24.pdf` + **`Fehlerbericht.txt`** ("Diese Rechnungen konnten nicht erstellt werden: R126-120"), the same batch holding both PDFs 26 s later (AC3). **Regenerating an existing draft opens no window** — the old stored file is served until the new one lands (~43 s) — so the two mutating tests are **env-gated** (`INVOICE_409_PRESCRIPTION_ID=<id>`): creating an invoice is irreversible, and the only copayment-eligible uninvoiced VO left is **6314-2, which is #3426's exclusion fixture and must never be invoiced**. **Traps:** the sweep uses `HEAD` (6x cheaper, and its `content-disposition` still names the stored file); nonexistent ids and a cancelled invoice under `type: copayment` are dropped from a bulk zip **silently**, never reported; and the statuses are deliberately distinct — **409** = stored file missing, **404 "This invoice has no Stornorechnung."** = no Storno. | `sa_invoice_export.spec.ts` | `@SuperAdmin @InvoiceExport @ReadOnly` | RC 3.12 (#3493) the **Rechnungsexport** screen at `/invoice-export`: one Gesellschaft + a date range → an on-screen table and a CSV of every invoice issued in that window. **Deployed; all 8 ACs verified live.** Three surfaces are compared directly — the FILE (`POST /invoices/audit/export` with **`{entity, from, to}`** — note `entity`, not `billingEntity`, and `from`/`to`, not the list's `issuedFrom`/`issuedTo`), the LIST behind the table (`GET /invoices?billingEntity=&issuedFrom=&issuedTo=&groups[]=invoice-list:read`, the screen's own request), and the SCREEN. **The headline evidence is the partition**: the seven per-Gesellschaft exports hold **all 550 invoices, each under exactly one Gesellschaft, 0 missing and 0 duplicated** — for a tax export "nothing was silently dropped" is the property that matters, and file-vs-list row sets match exactly for all 7. AC8 is proven on real data: all **10** cancelled invoices carry their Storno number and each Storno is its own **negative** row — and they are only in the file because the filter is `COALESCE(issueDate, createdAt)`: **every Storno document on staging has a NULL `issueDate`**, so an issueDate-only filter would drop all 10.
